## Canada added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Canada's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **2 works in 4 files: *Math First Peoples — Teacher Resource Guide, Elementary & Secondary* (FNESC/FNSA, ©2020), 273 pp. — the one book in Canada's classified in-hand Maths set (0 student + 1 teacher) — and the borderline pupil work *I Like Math!* (Government of Nunavut, 2017), one Level 8 guided-reading fiction book held in three language editions of 11 pp. each and filed in every record under the Inuktut Ilinniaqta Early Books label, not under Maths** — and placed 117 items (112 teacher-guide evidence items from the guide, items 1–112, plus 5 pupil items from *I Like Math!*, items S1–S5). Every item from the guide is TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE: the guide directs teachers how to teach BC mathematics through First Peoples contexts, with mini-units, activities and blackline masters, and states BC curriculum connections in its tables; it expressly does not explain the mathematics or the curriculum in detail (its printed p. 35) and presumes a trained teacher. No pupil Maths textbook is in hand for Canada: Canada has no national textbook series (education is provincial/territorial), and the five provincial student textbooks in the records (Nelson *Principles of Mathematics 10*; McGraw-Hill Ryerson *Principles of Mathematics 9*; Pearson *Math Makes Sense 3, 7, 8*) stand filled-by-exact-link only — never staged, never secured, not read here and cited nowhere (a sixth, *Nelson Mathematics 8*, is a candidate only, its imprint never read). Canada is therefore cited as EVIDENCE ONLY, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent: Canada is added to this lesson's countries line and named in its rows, but counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure. The *I Like Math!* items are stocked as pupil evidence from that work exactly as the placements label them — 3 carry taught content (S1, S2, S4) and 2 are context/named-only (S3, S5) — and are likewise counted in no taught-in figure; whether the Nunavut early-books titles count as pupil Maths at all is a record-side question for the parent, reported here, not ruled on. The guide's Water-Keepers items (98–105) rest on the guide's printed teaching alone: the separate FNESC Water Keepers digital resource the guide points to (printed p. 224) is in no record as in hand and was not read. The guide is the 2020 revision of the 2011 *Math First Peoples* resource; the 2011 predecessor located on a Padlet host is not a separate in-hand book and is cited nowhere. The placements' named exclusions — the guide's introduction, goals/rationale and teacher-reflection material; First Peoples Principles of Learning, story protocols and respectful-practice rules; the resource-design rubric; community-visit apparatus beyond the Interview Guide's six content categories; unit-organization front matter; the cultural/historical context narratives and stories inside the mini-units (context-setting only; no story text is reproduced anywhere in the placements or here); BLM worksheets that merely repeat an itemised activity; formative/summative assessment prompts, guiding questions, video and website lists and the Additional Resources bibliographies; and the *I Like Math!* front/back matter — teach no new mathematics and are stocked nowhere. The 5 book errors found in the read (placements E1–E5) are carried corrected on the rows they touch: E1 (sphere-volume worked example, printed p. 123 — a unit slip and a garbled intermediate line inside a volume calculation; the final answer, 500 cm³, is correct) on item 56; E2 (BLM 6 conversion sheet, printed p. 185 — the percent-to-decimal rule states the decimal-point direction backwards; both printed examples are correct) on item 83; E3 (Moose Tracks answer key, printed p. 181 — mixes “less than or equal to 20” with m < 20 and marks the boundary value inconsistently) on item 71; E4 (Circle Unit Answer Key, printed p. 211 — says “smoke hole” for “fire circle”; the project instructions at printed p. 204 state it correctly) on item 92; E5 (Weaving BLM list, printed p. 55 — numbers both Keep or Toss sheets BLM 16; they are BLM 15 and BLM 16) is a list typo affecting no item's content. Printed page = PDF page in the guide throughout; *I Like Math!* citations are its printed story folios. Canada's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**. Into this lesson the placements stock 5 items (items 11, 42, 56, 57 and 101).

**Book 1:** *Math First Peoples — Teacher Resource Guide, Elementary & Secondary* (teacher resource guide) — Canada, First Nations Education Steering Committee (FNESC) and First Nations Schools Association (FNSA), ©2020; 32,264,979 bytes (byte-exact against the record), 273 PDF pages. Printed page = PDF page throughout. TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide directs teachers how to teach BC mathematics through First Peoples contexts and states curriculum connections; it expressly does not explain the mathematics in detail (printed p. 35); it is not a pupil textbook.

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |
|---|---|
| Volume (space the box takes up) vs capacity (space it can hold) of the model bentwood box, and designing nesting boxes to stated volume/capacity | Canada — *Math First Peoples — Teacher Resource Guide* (FNESC/FNSA, 2020) (teacher guide) — teacher-guide evidence, printed pp. 46–47 |
| Curriculum connection stated by the guide (Math 8 big idea for the Masks and Poles units): the relationship between surface area and volume of 3D objects — connection table only | Canada — *Math First Peoples — Teacher Resource Guide* (FNESC/FNSA, 2020) (teacher guide) — teacher-guide evidence, printed pp. 101–102 |

Canada's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)** — the classified in-hand set is one teacher resource guide with no pupil Maths textbook in hand; the five exact-link provincial student textbooks were not read and are cited nowhere.

## Costa Rica added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Costa Rica's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **25 books, all of them student books: the five CONED distance-secondary antologías *Antología de Matemáticas*, Niveles 7–11 (UNED/CONED — Nivel Sétimo 2017, Nivel Noveno 2018, Nivel 11 2019; Nivel Décimo by MSc. Jorge Díaz Porras) and the ITCR Proyecto EVEPRIM primary books for Grades 3 and 6 (10 textos + 10 folletos de prácticas across Relaciones y Álgebra, Números, Geometría, Medidas, Estadística and Probabilidad); 0 teacher guides for Costa Rica Maths exist in hand** — and placed 229 teaching items. **SET CORRECTION: the expansion map's count of 17 student is WRONG** — 17 is exactly the number of Costa Rica Mathematics rows in textbooks.tsv (10 EVEPRIM textos + only 2 of the 10 EVEPRIM folletos + 5 CONED antologías); the TSV was never updated for 8 folletos staged on 2026-10-25, and the staging books.json files and phase3-push-state.json agree on the true set of 25 (the folletos count student-side, per the map's own stated basis). All 25 were grabbed, verified (byte size + MD5 against books.json; the two Relaciones y Álgebra textos, which have no books.json entry, verified by size + title page as the main-repo files) and read in full — no partial excerpts, no duplicates (the SharePoint folder copies of the six texto books matched the staged sizes byte-for-byte, so they are the same files, cited once) and no unopenable files. The three Grade 3 textos were read in their November 2026 editions, the in-hand editions per books.json; the GitHub release mid-size-books-3 still holds the superseded October editions of those three textos (a record-level flag, reported, not fixed — same books, older exports; only the November editions are cited here). Two chapter promises in the CONED books are NOT teaching and place no item anywhere: CONED 7's Capítulo 4 is titled Estadística y Probabilidad but teaches statistics only (probability appears only in its introductory discussion questions, pp. 209–214), and CONED 10's Capítulo 1 objectives promise plane transformations that no Tema in the chapter teaches (only a recall of Grade 8 homothety). The placements' named exclusions — front matter, indices, bibliographies and answer keys (soluciones); the EVEPRIM answer-key chapters and the review chapters that re-teach already-placed sections; the CONED chapter motto/introduction pages and “desafío matemático” pages; and the videos and web interactives the EVEPRIM books link to — are apparatus and are stocked nowhere. The 5 CONED book-error groups found in the read — CONED 7: the angle table (p. 106) labels a 360° angle “CONVEXO”, the inverse-proportionality example (pp. 201–203) states v·t = 247.5 for a product of 330 and “very close to 3 hours” at 78 km/h for ≈4.23 h with garbled fraction work, the triangle inequality (p. 125) omits “any two” sides, the median answer (p. 238) gives 68 for a median of 67, the worked powers page (p. 64) misaligns its results, and a tiling exercise (p. 35) reads “largo por largo”; CONED 8: the table of contents numbers the equations Tema “Tema 4” while the body heads it “Tema 5” (numbering slip only); CONED 9: the SI prefix table (p. 74) gives exa the symbol “R” (exa is E), the radian-to-degree rule (p. 117) is printed inverted/garbled though its worked examples are correct, and the contents duplicates “Función Cuadrática” for Capítulo 3 Temas 1 and 4 and mislabels Capítulo 4 as “Capítulo III”; CONED 10: the falling-body model (p. 373) prints “12” for ½ (the worked values use ½ correctly), the key (p. 42) duplicates “tangentes” where “secantes” is meant, p. 14 asks for the area of a “circunferencia” (the circle), and the pentagon solution (p. 86) drops its degree symbols; CONED 11: the logarithm table (p. 118) misaligns its examples (it prints log₅ 5 = 2, which belongs to log₅ 25), the wound-model solution (p. 172) shows an unsigned division display before the correct result, and “invectiva” (p. 121) is a typo for inyectiva — are carried corrected on the rows they touch. One further note is carried as the placements carry it: the Medidas G3 answer key's building-height estimate reads 45 dam in the October copy and “4 dam” in the November extraction — treated as a re-export/extraction artefact in the key, not teaching content, and cited nowhere. Costa Rica's books are pupil books, so Costa Rica is COUNTED: it joins this lesson's acceptance count wherever the placements stock an item. CONED printed page = PDF page throughout; EVEPRIM pages are cited by PDF page with the book's section numbers (two exceptions print folios: the EVEPRIM 6 Probabilidad texto's printed folio = PDF page − 6 and the EVEPRIM 3 Medidas texto's = PDF page − 7; citations give PDF pages). Into this lesson the placements stock 7 items (items 57, 81, 129, 155, 203, 220 and 221).

## Chile added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Chile's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **21 filed rows = 19 distinct books (11 student volumes + 8 teacher-guide volumes)**: MINEDUC *Textos del Estudiante* Matemática 1° básico to 2° Medio plus the combined 3°–4° Medio volume, and eight *Guías Didácticas del Docente*. Every file was grabbed through the authenticated GitHub API route and verified byte-for-byte — each file's size equals its GitHub Contents metadata and its locally computed git blob SHA-1 equals the blob SHA recorded by GitHub; no mirror copy was used for any of the 21 files (the standing warning about librosdelmineduc.cl mirror editions is settled for this held set). The files filed as Grade 12 (the student book and the teacher guide) are byte-identical to the Grade 11 files — MINEDUC issues this level as ONE combined 3°–4° Medio volume filed under both grades — so each is cited once, at Grade 11, and the set's headline is 19 distinct books behind 21 filed rows wherever the count is used. All 11 distinct student books are complete full-year books (index, all units and end matter — glossary / answer key / bibliography — verified in place in every volume) and their items are COUNTED normally. **Every held teacher guide is a TOMO 2 partial volume** — covering student Units 3–4 (Grade 4: Units 3–5; the combined 3°–4° Medio guide: the 4° Medio year only, its 3° Medio guidance sitting in the unheld Tomo 1); **no Tomo 1 is held for any grade**, so the guide items below are teacher evidence only, restricted to exactly the units each Tomo 2 contains, named in the rows and never in the acceptance count. ## Finland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Finland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **24 files / 23 distinct works of government-hosted open material (OPH and the aoe.fi Library of Open Educational Resources); Finland has NO national textbook series** — and placed 173 items (51 pupil + 122 teacher evidence). The records' kind labels are wrong for most of the set: 13 of the 18 Mathematics-labelled “student” files are actually teacher-facing (in-service course books, theory texts, activity series and instructor guides), and only 6 files are pupil-facing (the Metsämatikkaa cards, the quadrilateral chart, the weather game, the Päivänselvää chapter, the programming workbook and its model solutions) — every book below is carried at its TRUE kind, never the record label. One byte-identical duplicate (aoe.2674-alku = aoe.2686-alku, the same file under two aoe records) is cited once; the Päivänselvää book is a Chapter-2-only excerpt, cited only for what it holds; the quadrilateral chart's filed copy is 1 content page + 175 blank padding pages. The placements' named exclusions — the weather game's non-temperature content, the computing-pedagogy matter in the two Turku course books, the motivation and mindset material in the JoMa books, the Pikkumatikoita parts 2, 8 and 11, and the programming books' pure-programming tasks — are stocked nowhere. Pupil items are COUNTED normally; teacher-evidence items are named in the rows and stay outside the acceptance count. The Grade 1 Näppituntuma volume's appendices (printed pp. 37–41: number cards 1–100 and a hundred chart, dot cards, and < = > sign cards) are apparatus for its placed items and are stocked nowhere as content. Into this lesson the placements stock 2 pupil items and 1 teacher-evidence item from Book 1 and Book 2.

**Book 1:** *Näppituntuma — Alkuopetuksen matematiikkaa toiminnallisesti* (Grade 1 teacher book) — Finland, Opetushallitus (OPH) / Oulun Matikkamaa, Junttila & Ristola, 2011; 1,009,501 bytes, 43 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — an OPH supplementary teacher activity collection, expressly “not tied to any textbook”; Finland has no national textbook series.

**Book 2:** *Perusmatematiikkaa ohjelmoimalla* (pupil workbook, aoe.1371) — Finland, Järvenpää, 2021, ISBN 978-952-94-4547-9; 3,056,839 bytes, 62 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page. PUPIL material — counted. The three year-books (Grades 7, 8, 9) in one PDF (Grade 7 printed pp. 6–22, Grade 8 pp. 24–40, Grade 9 pp. 42–62). Its model solutions (*Perusmatematiikkaa ohjelmoimalla — Malliratkaisut*, aoe.1371, 31 pp.) mirror it task by task, confirm every placement below, and are cited wherever this book is cited. Pure-programming tasks (strings, lists, passwords, game mechanics) are stocked nowhere — only tasks whose own content is mathematics.

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read the Dominican Republic's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **14 books, all of them student books (0 teacher guides exist in hand)**: the 11 dedicated *Matemática* books of MINERD's Proyecto Libro Abierto, Serie 1 (*Matemática* 2°–6° Primaria and *Matemática* 1°–6° Secundaria), plus the mathematics content of the 3 integrated *Primero* volumes (Grade 1 has no dedicated *Matemática* book; only the three volumes' Matemática blocks are placed) — 3,184 PDF pages in all — and placed 661 teaching items (614 from the dedicated books, 47 from the Primero volumes). Every book was grabbed fresh by the authenticated GitHub API route, verified byte-exact against its staging `books.json` byte count and cover/title-proved, and the grabbed copy deleted after reading; the recorded Google Drive fallback is login-walled from this host and delivered no book. The expansion map's “16 student” count is wrong as a count of *Matemática* books: the dedicated set is 11, and 16 is reachable only by adding integrated all-subject files that are not *Matemática* books. The placements' named exclusions — the apparatus of all 14 books (unit-opening, group-activity, evaluation, self-assessment, bibliography and book-care pages, except the five group activities that teach a topic found nowhere else in their book, which are placed); the *Primero Volumen I* Matemática diagnostic evaluation; the Primero competency pages; the Primero volumes' non-maths sequences (with two maths-adjacent blocks inside them named in the placements and stocked nowhere); the 5 Nivel Inicial integrated books (a scope note, not part of the Maths set); and one record artefact that is not a book (a stray partial-upload `.part` file in the grade-04 push state) — are stocked nowhere. One book error is carried corrected on the row it touches: *Matemática 3° Primaria*'s weight table (evaluation, printed p. 152; the same table family at p. 147) pairs 8 lb with 48 oz — wrong: 1 lb = 16 oz, so 8 lb = 128 oz (48 oz is 3 lb). Minor slips, no action: the contents lists of *Matemática* 2° and 3° Secundaria run one page behind the printed folios — every citation uses the folios. Checked and NOT an error: *Matemática 4° Secundaria*, printed p. 84, prints Pythagoras as a² = b² + c² with a defined as the hypotenuse — correct as printed. The Dominican Republic's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand Maths book (the 11 dedicated *Matemática* books plus the Primero volumes carrying Grade 1's maths) has been read in full and placed.

## Gabon added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Gabon's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **17 documents: 6 pupil textbooks (the CIAM *Mathématiques* volumes for 6e, 5e, 4e, 2e Scientifique, 1re SM and 1re Littéraire, EDICEF), 6 teacher guides (the *Super Efficace* guides pédagogiques for 1re–5e année, EDIG/EDICEF, and the CIAM *Mathématiques 2e S* guide pédagogique) and 5 curriculum documents (the IPN *Curriculum Mathématiques* for 1re–5e année)** — every file grabbed fresh for the read, its byte size and page count matching the records exactly and its identity proved from cover/title/sommaire pages — and placed 522 items: 83 pupil-textbook teaching items, 389 teacher-evidence items and 50 curriculum-requirement items. The expansion map's total of 17 files is right but its split is wrong in kind (the read's flagged claim F1): the map lists “11 student + 6 teacher”, counting the 5 IPN curriculum PDFs among the student books; the true split is 6 pupil textbooks + 6 teacher guides + 5 curriculum documents. **No *Super Efficace* pupil book is in hand at any primary grade** — the primary pupil books are print-only commercial (EDIG) and were never secured, so Gabon's primary coverage is the IPN curriculum plus the teacher guide for each year; each guide teaches every lesson of the absent pupil book and cites its pages, and the guide rows below cite both the guide page and the pupil-book page the guide gives. There are no partial volumes and no duplicates in the set: every CIAM scan runs from its first chapter to its own index at the sommaire's stated page (the 6e scan opens at printed p. 3 — the cover and title leaves are not in the scan, but all teaching content is present, printed pp. 3–222 = the PDF's 220 pages exactly). The two misleading historic Drive filenames are settled by the books' own title pages: the file named “2nde SM” is the CIAM 2e Scientifique pupil book, and the file named “2nde C” is its 2e S Guide pédagogique. Link-only CIAM volumes (3e, Terminale SM/SE and their guides) are not in hand and are cited nowhere. Counting grain: one curriculum item per palier × compétence statement; one guide item per lesson the guide teaches; one pupil item per sommaire chapter. Book errors are carried corrected where they land: the Curriculum 2e année, Compétence 2, Palier 1 prints “le passionnément des objets l'espace”, a typo for “positionnement” (positioning of objects in space), and is stocked corrected; the Curriculum 3e année, Compétence 1, Palier 2 states “nombres de 1001 à 1999 (0 à 1999)” — the parenthetical contradicts the main range, and the statement is stocked by its main range; the CIAM 4e index spells PPMC as “PPCM” (trivial, noted for the record). The placements' named exclusions — the guides' remediation/application apparatus between paliers; the curricula's front matter and framework apparatus (rationale, timetables, competency-framework and assessment sections); the CIAM pupil books' end-of-chapter exercise sets, préfaces, authors' letters and closing indexes; the 2e S pupil book's Greek-alphabet page; the 2e S guide's synoptic programme tables — are stocked nowhere. Gabon stocks only evidence rows in this lesson — teacher-evidence and curriculum-requirement, with no pupil-textbook row — so it is named in the countries line and in the rows but is NOT added to this lesson's acceptance count. Into this lesson the placements stock 4 items (3 teacher-evidence, 1 curriculum-requirement) from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3.

## Ireland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Ireland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **9 pupil textbooks of the Text & Tests series (The Celtic Press / CJ Fallon): one English volume, Text & Tests 4 (New Edition), and eight Irish-language (Téacs & Trialacha) volumes** — and placed 570 items. Ireland has no government-published textbooks: every book in the set is a commercial in-use title held as a complete free copy. The English TT4 was read in phase3-staging (byte- and SHA-256-verified in place); the eight Irish volumes were re-grabbed from their recorded COGG (cogg.ie) source URLs, each landing byte-exact at its recorded size, every file's identity proved from its cover, copyright and contents pages. The expansion map's count is right: **9 student + 0 teacher** — the one further Ireland file in hand, *Dúchas 2 — Leabhar Scileanna*, is a Junior Cycle Irish-language (Gaeilge) skills book, not Maths, and is cited nowhere in this course. Extent, honestly: five whole-course books (TT1, TT2 GL, TT3, English TT4, TT6); three strand volumes complete as published but covering one strand only (TT4 Irish = Strand 2; TT5 Irish = Strand 1; TT7 Irish = Strand 5); and one supplement booklet (the TT5 Forlíonadh — deferred Strand 1 material and the Central Limit Theorem), complete as published, not a standalone textbook. Two record flags ride with the set (the read's flagged claims F1–F2, both about the library records, not the course): **F1** — 8 of the 9 PDFs are absent from their phase3-staging folders, which hold only books.json + CURRICULUM.md; all eight are secured in GitHub and were re-grabbed byte-exact from COGG for the read, and restoring them into staging is owed on the library side. **F2** — the held TT3 is the updated edition (2024) of the 2013 book, with an added Chapter 19, Inferential Statistics, that no record mentions; its three items are stocked from that chapter and TT3's book entry names the edition wherever it appears. The older strand books and the English TT4 New Edition teach much of the same Leaving Certificate Higher Level ground in two languages and editions (e.g. TT6 chs. 1–2 ≈ TT4 Eng chs. 1–2; TT7 ch. 2 ≈ TT4 Eng ch. 7): each book's row is stocked as its own citation — they are distinct held books, not duplicates — while Ireland itself is counted once in this lesson's taught-in figure. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching section of each book. The placements' named exclusions — every book's chapter tests (“Cuir triail ort féin”), tasks, learning-experience and review sections, chapter summaries and Answers sections, and every book's prefaces, keyword lists and contents pages — are stocked nowhere. Ireland COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Ireland book is a pupil textbook. Into this lesson the placements stock 8 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (TT1), Book 2 (TT2 GL), Book 3 (TT3), Book 4 (TT6).

Morris and Deborah Crean (English ©2018), ISBN 978-1-9997795-3-5; 17,831,033 bytes, 423 PDF pp. Junior Cycle, 1st year. Printed page = PDF page − 7.

Printed page = PDF page − 9.

## Fiji added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Fiji's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 pupil textbooks of the Ministry of Education / Curriculum Development Unit series, one for each of Years 1–13** — and placed 1,012 items. The expansion map's count is exact: staging `books.json` holds exactly 13 Mathematics entries (one per year, all `kind: student`) and `phase3-push-state.json` lists all 13 as pushed; no Maths teacher guide is in hand (the AQEP Maths Guides for Lower Primary exist only as AnyFlip reader copies in the download list — not in hand, not counted). The staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, so each book was re-fetched for the read — from the repo tree (Contents API) where the pusher had stored it as a blob, else from the book's recorded `source_url` (a Wayback Machine capture of the ministry file, Beacon Media, or Google Drive) — and the grabbed copies were deleted after their placements were written. The read's nine flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) `textbooks.tsv` carries only 9 of the 13 rows (Years 1, 2, 3, 4, 7, 10, 11, 12, 13) — Years 5, 6, 8 and 9 are staged and pushed but missing from the TSV; (2) the Year 2 scan duplicates printed p. 57 and printed p. 58 is absent, so the Money unit's Activity 12 is not in hand to read and is cited nowhere; (3) the Year 5 capture has no cover/title leaves — the file opens at the Strand 1 divider and the body runs complete, Strand 1 p. 1 to Strand 5 p. 98, its identity resting on the staged record and the body's own structure; (4) Year 4's folio sequence is irregular — folios 44–48 and 54 never appear and folios 42 and 55 each appear twice — but the leaf count closes exactly (85 printed numbers − 6 absent + 2 duplicated = 81 body leaves), every contents strand and unit is present and no activity breaks mid-stream at the gaps, so the evidence points to the printed book's own folio misprints rather than missing leaves, a judgement the file alone cannot prove; (5) Year 3's scan order is scrambled at the end (contents folio ii and printed pp. 46, 51, 52 sit at PDF pp. 76–80) and one activity page appears twice (folioed 47 and 51 — counted once), with folio tracking accounting for all of printed pp. 1–75; (6) Year 10 exists in two packagings — the staged Beacon Media file (9,099,595 bytes, 202 pp.) is the copy cited; an ECopies capture of the same book is 9,079,205 bytes / 200 pp.; (7) Year 12's first Wayback transfer arrived truncated (11,794,148 bytes, broken cross-reference table) and was discarded — a fresh download delivered the complete book (17,085,310 bytes, 284 pp.), which is what was read; (8) Year 1's contents lists Chance at p. 65 and Data at p. 66 where the folios are 64 and 65 — the body count closes exactly, so nothing is missing and the contents is one out; (9) Year 6's interior copyright and acknowledgement pages misname it the “Year 7” book — a copy-paste error in the book itself; its cover metadata, contents, running heads and body are Year 6 throughout, and it is read and placed as Year 6. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach — among them Year 3's conversion-table lines “1,000 cm = 1 km” (correct: 100,000 cm) and “10,000 mm = 1,000 m” (correct: 10 m), Year 4's banner “30 minutes = 1 hour” against its own body's 60, Year 5's “1 000 millimetres = 1 litre”, Year 9's contents “Common Multitudes” (for Common Multiples) and its (a × b)ᵐ = aᵐ × bⁿ misprint, and Year 1's count-in-twos strip printing 9 where 19 belongs; Year 10 carries its own printed errata list (pp. 201–202), whose corrections are noted in the items. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching point — a named concept, method, rule or worked skill with its printed page. The placements' named exclusions — strand divider pages; contents and acknowledgement pages; pure exercise sets and worked-example repeats of an item already placed; Year 1's “Checking my number” ten-frame reference page (printed p. 32); Year 3's duplicated capacity page (counted once); Year 4's closing multiplication-table chart (printed p. 86); Year 10's printed errata list (pp. 201–202, apparatus — its corrections ride in the items); Year 12's closing motivational quotes and exam tips (printed pp. 273–275); Year 13's bibliography and back matter — are stocked nowhere. Fiji COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Fiji book is a pupil textbook. Into this lesson the placements stock 22 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (*Mathematics Year 2*), Book 2 (*Mathematics Year 4*), Book 3 (*Year 5 Mathematics*), Book 4 (*Year 6 Mathematics Textbook*), Book 5 (*Year 7 Mathematics Textbook*), Book 6 (*Mathematics Year 8*), Book 7 (*Form 3 Mathematics Textbook*), Book 8 (*Mathematics for Year 11*).

Printed page = PDF page − 2. Scan defect: printed p. 57 appears twice (PDF pp. 59–60 are the same leaf) and printed p. 58 is absent — the Money unit's Activity 12 is not in hand and is cited nowhere.

**Book 2:** *Mathematics Year 4* — Ministry of Education, Curriculum Development Unit, Revised 2015 (© 2013); 96,814,549 bytes, 86 PDF pp.; image-only scan read from rendered pages. ## Honduras added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Honduras's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **21 files = 18 distinct works: 9 pupil textbooks (Grades 3–9, Grade 10 Matemática I and Grade 11 Matemática III BCH) and 12 teacher-guide files for Grades 1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 8, 9, 10 and 11, the Grade 8 guide held in two editions (2018 and the 2022 revision) and the Grade 1 and 2 guides each in two scans** — all from the Secretaría de Educación's PROMETAM / UPNFM series produced with JICA support — and placed 335 items (218 pupil-textbook items and 117 teacher-evidence items), plus 3 duplicate-edition records (placements items 241, 242 and 338) that are cited once with their works and carry no separate items. The expansion map's “9 student + 12 teacher” count is right: the staging `books.json` files, the `phase3-push-state.json` pushed set and `textbooks.tsv` agree on exactly these 21 files. There is no Grade 1–2 pupil textbook in the Secretaría's programme (workbook only — SE Manual de Lineamientos), so the Grade 1–2 teaching in hand is the two guides; there is no Grade 3 or Grade 6 teacher guide in hand. Every file was grabbed from its recorded `source_url` (educatrachos.hn / libroshonduras.com) after the staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, and verified by exact byte size against the download-list record and by page count against the `books.json` record before reading; grabbed copies were deleted after each book's text was extracted. Read basis, stated honestly: the six text-layer pupil books and eight text-layer guides were read in full from their text; the three scanned pupil books (Grades 7–9) were read by OCR of their Índices and unit/lesson openers with interior pages OCR-sampled — Grades 7 and 8 cross-checked against the full text-layer read of their own teacher guides, which plan every lesson class by class — and the two 2022 guides are themselves scans, OCR-read at cover, presentation and index level, the Grade 9 guide therefore placed at unit grain. Counting grain: one item per Índice lesson; a lesson teaching two distinct course topics is split into two items, and three items (a Grade 4 pupil lesson, a Grade 10 pupil lesson and a Grade 2 guide unit) each carry a row in two lessons below and are counted once. The placements' named exclusions — the “Páginas para recortar” cut-out pages of the Grades 3, 4 and 5 pupil books (manipulatives, not teaching text), the Grade 6 book's closing “Repaso” review, and the “Nos divertimos” / “¿Sabías que?” enrichment boxes inside placed lessons — are stocked nowhere. The read's flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) the download list §A's original 2026-10-21 mirror labels for the III-Ciclo files were swapped between Español and Matemáticas — the files it calls “Matemáticas 7/8/9 (2018 PROMETAM)” are the Español books, and its “Español 7/8/9” alternates are the Matemáticas books — a library-record fault, already corrected in `books.json`, reported here and not fixed by this merge; (2) the Grade 9 guide's cover misprints its cycle as “II Ciclo” where the programme is III Ciclo; (3) the Grade 11 pupil book's Índice misprints “Cincunferencias” for “Circunferencias”, taught corrected; (4) the 2014 scan of the Grade 1 guide has a materially poorer text layer than the mirror scan of the same work, so anyone text-mining it alone will under-read it. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach. Into this lesson the placements stock 4 items — 3 pupil-textbook rows and 1 teacher-evidence row — from Book 4 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 6to grado*), Book 17 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 9no grado*), Book 18 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas and 9no grado (2022)*).

**Book 4:** *Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 6to grado* — Honduras, Secretaría de Educación (PROMETAM / UPNFM series, JICA support); 19,879,660 bytes, 172 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page − 6. Pupil textbook.

**Book 17:** *Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 9no grado* — Honduras, Secretaría de Educación; the mirror copy, 37,650,026 bytes, 190 PDF pp. (not the se.gob.hn file of the same filename, which is the Español 9 book). Scanned-image PDF with no text layer: read by OCR of its Índice and unit/lesson openers, with interior pages OCR-sampled. Printed page = PDF page − 4. Pupil textbook.

**Book 18:** *Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 9no grado* (2022) (teacher guide) — Honduras, Secretaría de Educación; 514,454,953 bytes, 248 PDF pp. Scanned-image PDF with no text layer: read by OCR at cover, presentation and index level, and placed at unit grain because its lesson-level index sits in the scanned class-plan pages; the lessons it plans are exactly Book 17's lessons. Teacher evidence only. Book error carried: its cover prints “II Ciclo” for this Grade 9 guide, where the programme is III Ciclo (Grades 7–9) — a misprint on the book's own cover; its Presentación and the DCNEB place Grade 9 in III Ciclo.

## Italy added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Italy's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **14 pupil textbooks, all adopted commercial titles (Italy has no government textbooks; schools adopt titles under the AIE/MIUR adozioni system): the Bergamini *Matematica.blu* volumes (Zanichelli) and the Sasso *Colori della Matematica* Edizione Verde and Edizione Blu volumes and triennio modules A–G (Petrini / De Agostini Scuola)** — and placed 781 items. The expansion map's “14 student + 0 teacher” count and kind split are exact against the staging `books.json` files, `textbooks.tsv` and the push state; every file was verified against its record (byte size, and MD5 where the record carries one) and read in full. Counting grain: one item per numbered theory section of the book's own Indice/Sommario. Two extent faults ride with the set, honestly: the files filed as *Matematica.blu* Vol. 1 (Book 3) and *Matematica.blu 2.0* Vol. 5 (Book 4) are each only the SECOND PART of the volume — Vol. 1 opens at Capitolo 13 (printed p. 670) and its Capitoli 1–12 and G1–G4 are not in the file; Vol. 5 opens at Capitolo 25 (printed p. 1560) and its Capitoli 18–24 are not in the file — so both are named as partial volumes wherever they are cited, only their held content is placed, and the missing first parts are not in hand anywhere and are cited nowhere (the ground the Vol. 1 first part would have covered is supplied inside this set by Books 5–6 at Sasso depth, and the Vol. 5 first part's limits and continuity ground by Book 12, Modulo G). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the eBook-only units the books' own Indici mark “(solo nell'eBook)”; and the apparatus around the numbered theory sections in every book — chapter openings, exercise blocks, laboratories, *Realtà e modelli*, exam-preparation sections (*Verso l'esame*, *Verso l'INVALSI*, *Verso l'università*), glossaries, answer keys, formula sheets, and *Approfondimento* boxes that are not numbered theory sections. Into this lesson the placements stock 9 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (*Matematica.blu — Vol. 2*), Book 2 (*Matematica.blu 2.0 — Vol. 4*) and Book 10 (*Colori della Matematica — Edizione Blu, Modulo F*).

**Book 1:** Bergamini/Trifone/Barozzi, *Matematica.blu — Vol. 2: Algebra, Geometria, Probabilità* (Zanichelli, 2010; filed at Grade 10) — Italy; 12,160,155 bytes, 907 PDF pp., MD5 identical to the source record's. The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Mozambique’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **15 files: 14 pupil Matemática modules of the ministry’s PESD distance-learning programme (PESD I Módulos 1–5, studied by the 1º ciclo = grades 7–9; PESD II Módulos 1–9, studied by the 2º ciclo = grades 10–12) and 1 teacher guide, the *Manual do Professor — 1.ª Classe*, whose Matemática part (printed pp. 146–183) is teacher evidence** — all from the Ministério da Educação e Desenvolvimento Humano (MINEDH) through its Instituto de Educação Aberta e à Distância (IEDA) — and placed 233 items (227 pupil-textbook items and 6 teacher-evidence items). Mozambique’s basis, by the user’s 2026-11-02 ruling, is this ministry distance-learning (PESD) set, not the commercial print set: the modules are studied by cycle, not per grade, and are cited as distance-learning books wherever they appear. The expansion map’s “14 student” count is exact, but its “0 teacher” is wrong — the teacher guide is filed under subject “general” at grade-01 and carries a full Matemática part; the true in-hand set is 14 pupil + 1 teacher guide. The three staging `books.json` files, `textbooks.tsv` and `phase3-push-state.json` agree on exactly these 15 files; no staging PDF remained on local disk (pusher-secured, local copies deleted), so every file was fetched fresh from its recorded official source, ieda.gov.mz, and matched its recorded byte size exactly; all 15 MD5s are distinct — no duplicates — and the generically named PESD II file `Modulo-9.pdf` is proved by its title page to be Matemática Módulo 9. Printed-page basis: PESD I modules — printed page = PDF page − 1 (footer-verified); PESD II modules — printed page = the contents-page foliation; the *Manual do Professor* — printed page = PDF page. Counting grain: one item per lesson of a module where the lesson teaches one course topic; a lesson is split into two items where it teaches two clearly distinct course topics. Book errors found on the read are taught corrected, as the placements record them: PESD II Módulo 3’s cover subtitle wrongly repeats Módulo 1’s (“Conjuntos Numéricos e Cálculo Algébrico”) — its interior is exponential and logarithmic functions throughout — and its answer section is headed “Soluções Modulo 1” (sic) although it is Módulo 3’s own key; PESD II Módulo 9’s answer section is headed “Soluções Módulo 8” (sic) although it is Módulo 9’s own key; PESD II Módulo 5’s contents numbers two different lessons “Lição 4” (the linear-function revision, printed p. 43, and the modulus function, printed p. 55) — both lessons are placed, as items 177 and 178 of this read; and PESD I Módulo 4’s contents page omits its Lição 13, which exists in the body at printed p. 64 and is placed (item 96), while its Lições 14 and 15 carry identical stated objectives and are placed as two items as printed (items 97 and 98). The placements’ record items are apparatus, stocked nowhere: R1 — every PESD module’s closing preparation test (teste de preparação), answer key (chave de correcção / soluções) and bibliography; R2 — the *Manual do Professor*’s answer key to the 1.ª Classe pupil book (printed pp. 174–183; the pupil book itself is not in hand); R3 — the DGD Módulo 1 spot-check copy, fetched only to verify its subject identity. The placements’ named exclusions are stocked nowhere: DGD (Desenho e Geometria Descritiva, PESD II Módulos 1, 3–12 — a separate named subject of the Mozambican curriculum with its own module series, interior-checked on Módulo 1 and excluded as such; Módulo 2 is not in hand at all); all other PESD subjects in hand (Biologia, Química, Física, Geografia, História, Inglês, Português, Francês, TIC, Agro-pecuária, Empreendedorismo, Educação Visual, Filosofia) — separate curriculum subjects, none a Matemática book in disguise; the *Manual do Professor*’s Língua Portuguesa and Educação Física parts (printed pp. 4–145 and 184–215); and the primary print-programme Matemática books (1ª–6ª, Edição revista 2025), which are link-only, not in hand and not read. Pupil items are COUNTED normally; the teacher-evidence items (T1–T6, from the one teacher guide) are named in the rows and stay outside the acceptance count. The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Nauru's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **10 documents, all of them Learning Through Doing (LTD) *Mathematics Planning Documents*, Nauru editions (2026) — Prep and Years 1–8, plus the LTD *Scope and Sequence, Years 1–8* — teacher planning alignments that sequence an Australian commercial subscription lesson system against the Nauru Mathematics Syllabus, every lesson keyed to a Nauru curriculum code (NRU-*); 0 pupil (student) textbooks, 0 teacher guides, 0 workbooks** — and placed 238 items, every one of them PLANNING/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE: the Nauru syllabus's own key-idea statements as printed on each document's closing “Nauru Curriculum Alignment” pages. Nauru publishes no government textbooks (country record, download list §§F–Q, seven fronts), so Nauru's standing in the course is the syllabus-evidence reckoning: Nauru is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure and is named in the countries line and in the rows below only, on the standing evidence-only precedent (Bahamas / Canada / Myanmar). The expansion map's count is right in number and wrong in kind: it carries Nauru as “10 student Maths books”, but every one of the 10 books.json entries is kind `planning-document` and states in its own approval note that it is NOT a Nauru government student textbook (diff fault F1 — a record-side fault, reported, not fixed by this merge). textbooks.tsv carries 12 Nauru rows, all of them USP IOE ESD items, and zero Mathematics rows — the 10 planning documents live only in books.json and push-state. Australia overlap is ZERO: the Australia read's nine Cambridge/Jacaranda Years 8–10 pupil textbooks share no title, ISBN, byte size or file with this set, and the QCE senior titles cross-referenced in Nauru's country record are link-only, in hand for neither country and read by neither reader; nothing from the Australia placements is cited here. All 10 files were read from local disk in phase3-staging, byte-exact against their books.json records (334,327–887,725 bytes; 67 PDF pages in total; 10 distinct MD5s, no duplicates inside the set); nothing was downloaded. Printed-page basis: the documents carry no printed folios (the Scope's footer “Page N” equals its PDF page), so pages cited are document (PDF) pages — Prep key ideas on alignment pp. 6–7 of 7, Years 1–8 on alignment p. 6 of 6; the term overviews (p. 1) and weekly plans (pp. 2–5) sequence the same key ideas and add none of their own. Counting grain: one item per NRU key idea. The *Scope and Sequence* (887,725 bytes, 13 PDF pages) was read in full and cross-checked — every Scope topic traces to a year document's lessons under the codes placed — so it contributes 0 items of its own; counting it again would double-count one programme as two sources. Book errors found on the read are placed corrected, as the placements record them (diff fault F2): Year 3 NRU-M3.3 prints “suing” for “using” and NRU-G3.3 prints “jey” for “key”; Year 5 NRU-CD5.4 prints “recoding sheets” for “recording sheets”; Year 8 NRU-PA8.4 prints “co-ordiinates”; the Year 8 alignment page prints its first geometry code as “NRU-G9.1” — a Year 9 code inside the Year 8 document — while the Year 8 weekly plans cite the same lessons as NRU-G8.1, and item 226 is placed as the Year 8 item it is; the Year 2 document's Term 4 plan cites “NRU-N3.4” against Year 2 strategy-review lessons, where the alignment page's NRU-N2.4 governs; several weekly-plan code strings are garbled in print — the alignment pages' code lists are clean and govern throughout. The placements also record a document inconsistency (diff fault F3): the Scope's fractions table opens its halves entry at the earliest (Prep) level, but the Prep planning document contains no fractions anywhere — fractions enter the year documents at Year 1 (NRU-N1.8) — and the placements follow the year documents. Some key ideas are explicitly Nauru-localised (Year 3 NRU-G3.3: simple maps of Nauru; Year 6 NRU-CD6.4: data contexts “familiar to Nauruan students”; Year 8 NRU-PA8.6: graphs “using data from Nauru”). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the Nauru Mathematics Syllabus aims paragraph printed in every year document (general aims, no subject content); the term overviews and weekly plans as separate items (they sequence the same key ideas, and the LTD lesson titles are the Australian system's commercial catalogue, delivered by subscription — the planning documents do not reproduce the lessons, so no lesson content is placed, only the syllabus key ideas the lessons are sequenced against); “Assessment Opportunities” headers and blank assessment columns (apparatus); the Scope & Sequence's duplicated topic statements across years (one programme, counted once at key-idea grain); the 12 USP IOE ESD files filed under Nauru (not Mathematics, excluded by subject); and the OER4Pacific COL open-schooling Mathematics 11/12 PDFs named in the download list §G (regional OER, never staged under Nauru, not in hand, not read). Into this lesson the placements stock 5 planning/curriculum-evidence items from Book 1 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 2, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 2 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 5, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 3 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 7, Nauru edition (2026)*), Book 4 (*LTD Mathematics Planning Document, Year 8, Nauru edition (2026)*).

## Nigeria added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Nigeria’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **11 files: 1 pupil textbook and 10 teacher guides** — and placed 199 items (20 pupil-teaching items from the pupil textbook and 179 teacher-evidence items from the ten guides). The pupil textbook is *New Concept Mathematics for Senior Secondary Schools 1* (3rd ed. 2016, Learn Africa, 352 pp., 19,071,603 B), held as a release asset in `mid-size-books-8`; the guides are 7 Learn Africa guides (New Method Primary 1; New Concept JSS 1–3 and SS 1–3) and 3 Cambridge *Excellence in Mathematics* teacher’s guides (Books 1–3). The expansion map’s kind split (0 student + 10 teacher + 1 link-only) is wrong on the student count — the SS1 pupil textbook is in hand, and the map’s “1 link-only” is plausibly this same book misclassified; the true split is 1 student + 10 teacher, 11 files (flag F1, record-side). Record discrepancy (flag F2, record-side): the records describe the held JSS 1 teacher’s guide as a 2024 file of 396,871 B (4th edition), but the file in hand and read is the **2026 fifth edition** (362,534 B, ISBN 978 978 925 9755); the 2024 file is not in hand. Book errors found on the read are taught corrected (flag F3): the JSS 1 guide’s Ch20 states “1 hour = 1°” on the clock face — 1 hour = 30°; the JSS 2 guide prints “99 300 = 9.03 × 10⁴” (should be 9.93 × 10⁴), works a simple-interest example stated with T = 4 years at 3 years, and gives the Pythagorean triple “(5, 12, 23)” (should be (5, 12, 13)); the SS 1 guide prints the sector-area formula as θ/360 × 2πr² (should be θ/360 × πr²) and describes standard-form A as “between 0 and 9” (should be 1 ≤ A < 10); the SS 2 guide works log 0.000 592 and prints the characteristic without its negative/bar sign, and conflates “complementary” with “independent” events in places; the SS 3 guide calls A⁻¹ “the negative matrix” in one section (it is the inverse matrix, used correctly later), with several standard-integral lines and one index law garbled in print. The Primary 1 guide’s printed Contents page numbers do not match its body folios (flag F4) — its items cite body folios throughout. Printed-page basis: Learn Africa guide items cite the guide’s own printed (folio) pages — chapter starts from each book’s contents, verified against body folios; pupil-book items cite the pupil book’s printed pages; Cambridge items cite the guide’s printed topic-start page, each topic’s Student’s Book page span being given in the book’s scheme of work. Counting grain: one item per teaching chapter (Learn Africa books) or per teaching topic (Cambridge guides). The placements’ named exclusions are stocked nowhere: revision and exam-practice apparatus across the series (the SS1 pupil book’s Preparatory chapter and its three Revision-exercise parts; Cambridge TG1 Topics 1 and 16, TG2 Topics 1 and 18, TG3 Topics 1 and 13 and TG3’s sample examination papers); the SS 3 guide’s Ch16 “Numbers and numeration”, an explicit revision chapter; reference apparatus (the pupil book’s Tables and formulae, Four-figure tables, Answers and Index; the Cambridge guides’ Introduction sections); and the non-Maths Nigeria files checked and excluded (the English, Economics, Government, Commerce, Biology, Chemistry, Physics and Literature books in hand, the FME approved-textbooks list, and the 6 TVET trades curriculum documents — none is a Maths book). Pupil items are COUNTED normally; teacher-guide items are worded as teacher evidence and stay outside the acceptance count. Into this lesson the placements stock 6 items — 1 pupil-textbook row and 5 teacher-evidence rows — from Books 2, 4, 5, 6, 7 and 10.

**Book 2:** *New Concept Mathematics for JSS 1 — Teacher's Guide*, 5th edition 2026 — Nigeria, Learn Africa, ISBN 978 978 925 9755; 362,534 bytes, 56 PDF pp. Teacher guide — teacher evidence only. The records describe the held copy as a 2024 file of 396,871 B (4th edition); that file is not in hand — the held and read copy is this 2026 fifth edition, and the placements are from it.

**Book 4:** *New Concept Mathematics for JSS 3 — Teacher's Guide*, 4th edition 2023 — Nigeria, Learn Africa, ISBN 978 978 925 8017; 415,117 bytes, 44 PDF pp. Teacher guide — teacher evidence only.

**Book 5:** *New Concept Mathematics for Senior Secondary Schools 1*, 3rd edition 2016 — Nigeria, Learn Africa, ISBN 978 978 925 280 0; 19,071,603 bytes, 352 PDF pp. Pupil textbook — held as the release asset `grade-10-approved-private-nigeria-mathematics-student-textbook.pdf` in release `mid-size-books-8`. Citations are the pupil book's printed pages.

**Book 6:** *New Concept Mathematics for SS 1 — Teacher's Guide*, 4th edition 2024 — Nigeria, Learn Africa, ISBN 978 978 925 8253; 409,134 bytes, 44 PDF pp. Teacher guide — teacher evidence only.

**Book 7:** *Excellence in Mathematics — Teacher's Guide Book 1* — Nigeria, Cambridge University Press, 2016, ISBN 978-1-316-60873-9; 2,950,777 bytes, 158 PDF pp. Teacher guide — teacher evidence only. Items cite the guide's printed topic-start page; each topic's Student's Book span (pp. 1–281) is given in the book's scheme of work.

**Book 10:** *New Concept Mathematics for SS 3 — Teacher's Guide*, 4th edition 2024 — Nigeria, Learn Africa, ISBN 978 978 925 8277; 589,159 bytes, 48 PDF pp. Teacher guide — teacher evidence only.

## Poland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Poland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDF — **1 document, the *Egzamin ósmoklasisty 2025 — Matematyka* (Grade 8 final examination, main session), Centralna Komisja Egzaminacyjna (CKE), paper code OMAP-100-2505, sat 14 May 2025; 1,483,019 bytes, 20 PDF pages — an EXAM PAPER, not a textbook: it teaches nothing** — and placed 15 examination items (the paper's closed tasks 1–15). Every Poland item in this section is therefore EXAMINATION EVIDENCE — the task assesses what a Polish Grade 8 pupil must be able to do; the paper teaches no content — cited as the evidence kind it is; Poland is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing evidence-only precedent (Ghana / Barbados), until a Polish pupil Maths textbook is in hand and read. Poland holds NO pupil Maths textbook, workbook or teacher guide in hand at all: the expansion map's count of one Maths file is right but its kind is wrong — it records this file as “1 student”, where textbooks.tsv's own kind column says `exam` and the file's cover is a timed exam booklet (125 minutes, 21 tasks, answer card) — the true split is student 0 · teacher 0 · exam 1 (a record-side fault, reported, not fixed by this merge; no maths/books/poland.md filing exists and none was created). The file is a PARTIAL record of the exam it names: printed pp. 1–17 = PDF pp. 1–17 carry the closed tasks 1–15 in full, but the open tasks 16–21 are NOT printed in this file — printed p. 16 carries only a placeholder per task (“ZADANIE N. ZNAJDUJE SIĘ NA KARCIE ROZWIĄZAŃ ZADAŃ OTWARTYCH”) with its point value (16: 0–2 · 17: 0–3 · 18: 0–2 · 19: 0–2 · 20: 0–3 · 21: 0–3); their statements live on the separate open-solutions card (8 pages, per the cover instruction), which is not in this file and not in hand — placements record item R1: tasks 16–21 are named only, no placement is made for any of them and no topic is inferred from the point values; if the card is ever secured, tasks 16–21 are owed a read and placements then. Printed-page basis: printed “Strona N z 17” = PDF page N for pp. 1–17 (verified on pp. 2, 4, 6, 8, 10, 12, 14, 16). Counting grain: one item per closed task (tasks 1–15); the two-part tasks state both assessed parts inside the item. The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the cover/instruction pages (printed pp. 1–2) and the “tasks begin on the following pages” page (printed p. 3) — exam apparatus, no assessed content; the draft pages “Brudnopis (nie podlega ocenie)” (printed pp. 5, 7, 9, 11, 13, 15, 17) — blank working space, explicitly not marked; PDF pp. 18–19 (blank) and PDF p. 20 (three “MATEMATYKA / Egzamin ósmoklasisty / E8” sticker labels) — answer-card apparatus. The hidden-maths check was proved on all 6 in-hand Poland files, not assumed: the Grade 8 English and Polish exam papers of the same session contain no mathematics, and the *Ars longa. Przemiany sztuki* Tom I–III (Grades 9–11 — the only pupil textbooks in Poland's in-hand set, Art History, not Maths) carry the word “matematyka” only incidentally (Tom I once, Tom II three times — a Leonardo quotation, Renaissance proportion, two biographical notes; Tom III zero occurrences) — no maths teaching hides under those labels, and none of the five is cited here. The read copy was grabbed by the authenticated GitHub API route at the exact recorded byte size (the file lives only as a blob in the textbooks repo tree; phase3-staging holds no Poland folder and phase3-push-state holds no Poland key) and deleted after the read. Everything else Poland-Maths in the library records (the 54 ZPE e-textbook readers, the WSiP flipbook readers, and Scribd / SlideShare / silo.tips / epage.pub candidates) is link-only — not in hand, outside this read, and no textbook can be claimed from it. Into this lesson the placements stock 1 examination-evidence item from Book 1.

## Jamaica added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Jamaica's whole in-hand Maths set from the files — **8 documents: 7 National Standards Curriculum (NSC) guides that carry a Mathematics section (Grades 1, 2 and 3 Integrated Studies / Language Arts / Mathematics; Grades 4, 5 and 6 Exploratory Core; and the Grades 7–9 Mathematics guide — all Ministry of Education, Youth and Information, © 2018, the Grade 6 guide in its FINAL edition of Jan 08 2020), plus 1 pupil reader** — and placed 311 items: 308 curriculum-evidence items from the guides and 3 pupil-text items from the reader. **Kind split: 0 pupil textbooks, 0 teacher guides, 7 curriculum guides.** A curriculum guide states what is REQUIRED to be taught and how to teach it; it is not a pupil textbook, so every guide item below is CURRICULUM EVIDENCE, worded “curriculum requires”, and Jamaica is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure. The expansion map is wrong on both count and kind (flag F1, record-side, reported not fixed): it listed Jamaica as FILES-IN-HAND with 4 student / 0 teacher Maths books — its build matched the subject field only, counting the three Grades 1–3 guides and the Grades 7–9 guide and missing the three Grades 4–6 Exploratory Core guides, whose subject field reads only “Exploratory Core” while their title pages name Mathematics as one of the four core subjects; and the four files it did count are curriculum guides, not student books. Two further record faults ride with the set (record-side; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records, and no maths/books/jamaica.md filing exists or was created): textbooks.tsv has **no row at all for the Grades 7–9 Mathematics guide** (flag F2), although the file is in staging, in books.json and in the pushed set; and the TSV's Grade 5 row points at a Telegram link instead of the file's secured GitHub asset (flag F3) — the book was read byte-exact from that asset. One file defect, stated plainly: the Grade 5 guide's body font has a broken ToUnicode map, so its text layer extracts as bullet glyphs; its Mathematics section was read in full from rendered page images (120-DPI renders of PDF pp. 139–255 = printed pp. 122–238), every page legible, and it is counted as read on that basis. Printed-page basis: each guide prints its own folio; the Mathematics windows run printed = PDF − 5 (Grade 1), − 33 (Grade 2), − 16 (Grade 3), − 11 (Grade 4), − 17 (Grade 5) and − 12 (Grade 6), and Book 7's pages are its own per-grade section pages. Counting grain: Grades 1–3, one item per distinct numbered objective in the Mathematics Content Standards, plus NEW/Extended objectives and Scope-and-Sequence topics stated in no standard (marked (S&S)); Grades 4–6 and 7–9, one item per distinct objective in each Mathematics unit's objectives list; an objective restated verbatim is counted once, at its first full statement. Boundary files examined and excluded — stocked nowhere: the nursery *Birth to Three is Key* early-childhood guide (ages 0–3, all-domains; number concepts are one activity focus, with no Mathematics section or objectives at NSC grain — not a Maths book); the Grades 4–6 Resource & Technology guide (its file is a bundle whose opening leaves are the Grades 7–9 English guide; no Mathematics section); and the Civics (×2), English Language, Integrated Science and Social Studies guides (no Mathematics subject in any of them; the Science guide's measurement/graphing is science content). The placements' named exclusions inside the books are likewise stocked nowhere: the Grade 1 window's “Maths Prayers” (printed pp. 242–243 — devotional poems, religious apparatus, no mathematics content); the Scope and Sequence charts, Process Strand checklists, Illustrative Activities, Benchmarks and Illustrative Examples as apparatus (topics stated only there are placed as items and marked (S&S)); the Integrated Studies and Language Arts windows of the Grades 1–3 guides, which restate the same Mathematics attainment targets as delivery vehicles and state no requirement beyond the window's standards; the Grade 1 book's Mathematics lesson plans and “Teaching Mathematics: Ideas & Activities” (printed pp. 340–400, teacher apparatus); Book 7's Appendices A–E and closing sample lesson plan (printed pp. 191–206, activity apparatus and worked examples of the same objectives); and the reader's copyright/credits leaves. Book errors and faults are carried as the placements record them, taught corrected where they touch a row: the Grade 1 fractions standard (printed p. 275) cross-refers an objective as “Objective 2.3.27” — a Grade 2 bank number inside the Grade 1 book — and objective numbers with a leading “2.” recur throughout the Grade 1 standards (the bank numbering is shared across grades; each such objective is placed once, as stated for Grade 1); the Grade 2 addition example on printed p. 268 (“Pat has 43 oranges … How many apples Pat has now?”) mixes fruits mid-problem — a wording slip, the objective it illustrates placed as read; two Grade 2 standards pages carry a mojibake line (a Caesar-shifted “See previous page for Attainment Targets and Objectives”) — a print-production artefact, no content lost; the Grade 3 window and its Scope and Sequence state **no decimals** anywhere, so no Grade 3 decimals row is stocked; the Grade 4 Term 3 Unit 2 outcome names “cross-sections” of cubes/cuboids, which the unit nowhere teaches — placed inside its item as named-only; the Grade 4 probability unit's header prints “GRAGE 4” (sic); the Grade 5 Overview disagrees with its own units three times, recorded as printed (division of five-digit numbers sits in Term 3 Unit 1, not Term 2; the Overview's Volume has no objective anywhere in the Grade 5 Mathematics section and is stocked nowhere; lines of symmetry sit in Term 2 Unit 3, while the Term 3 Geometry unit is circles, polygons to eight sides, and pyramids); the Grade 6 Term 3 Unit 1 tyre-pressure activity is Science-linked and its arithmetic is already inside its unit's item, not placed separately; in Book 7, the Grade 9 ratio unit's footer prints “NSC Mathematics: Grade 7” (a template error — the unit sits in the Grade 9 section and is placed as Grade 9), the matrices unit header prints “GRADE 9 TERM 1: NUMBERS” although the unit sits in the Term 3 sequence (placed as Term 3), the Grade 7 constructions unit's objectives page repeats the transformations unit's first objective (counted once, in the transformations item), and the Grade 8 Term 2 geometry unit carries a header title copied from the algebra unit while its objectives are polygons and angle sums (placed by its objectives). Jamaica's pupil Maths textbooks are commercial (Carlong / Hodder / Macmillan etc.) — the library holds none; they stand in the download list only, not in hand and not read. Into this lesson the placements stock 2 curriculum-evidence rows from Book 6 and Book 7.

| Grade 6 curriculum requires using appropriate units in measurement situations, calculating area and volume, differentiating between volume and surface area, constructing solids, and solving problems involving areas, perimeters, polygons and solid shapes | Jamaica — *Grade 6 NSC Guide: Exploratory Core* (curriculum guide) — curriculum evidence, printed p. 278 |
| Grade 8 Term 2 Measurement curriculum requires deriving and using the formulae for the area of parallelograms, triangles (½bh) and trapezia; computing the total surface area of cubes, cuboids, cylinders and triangular prisms; performing conversions within units up to cubed units; and estimating and calculating the volume and capacity of cubes, cuboids, prisms, cylinders and composite objects | Jamaica — *Grades 7–9 NSC Guide: Mathematics* (curriculum guide) — curriculum evidence, printed p. 95 |

Jamaica enters this lesson as curriculum evidence only: it is named in the countries line and in the rows above, but it is NOT added to the acceptance count above — Jamaica's in-hand Maths set holds no pupil textbook and no teacher guide, only the seven National Standards Curriculum guides, which state what must be taught without being pupil teaching material themselves (the standing evidence-only precedent). Jamaica's class for Maths is PARTIAL (evidence-only): every in-hand document has been read in full and stocked, and it advances only when a Jamaica pupil Maths textbook lands in hand and is read.
